### Hard Audit Protocol

#### 1. Disjunction Validation (P or Q)

- **P or Q: Mr. Green moved from the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM to the Hall at 12:00 AM.**
  - Mr. Green was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM and moved to the Hall at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **True (P)** or **True (Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True (P).

- **P or Q: Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM.**
  - Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **True (P)** or **True (Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True (P).

- **P or Q: Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.**
  - Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
  - Verify: **True (P)** or **True (Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True (P).

- **P or Q: Mr. Boddy was murdered at 12:00 AM.**
  - Mr. Boddy was murdered at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **True (P)** or **True (Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True (P).

#### 2. Biconditional Audit (P if and only if Q)

- **P if and only if Q: Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM if and only if the Rope was in the Hall at 12:00 AM.**
  - Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM and the Rope was in the Hall at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **True (P and Q)** or **False (P or Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True/True.

- **P if and only if Q: The Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM if and only if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.**
  - Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM and the Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **True (P and Q)** or **False (P or Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: True/True.

- **P if and only if Q: Mr. Green was in the Courtyard at 12:00 AM if and only if the Revolver was in the Dining Room at 12:00 AM.**
  - Mr. Green was in the Courtyard at 12:00 AM and the Revolver was not in the Dining Room at 12:00 AM.
  - Verify: **False (P and Q)** or **True (P or Q)**.
  - **Outcome**: False (P and Q).

#### 3. Contradiction Search

- **Contradiction with previous clues:**
  - Mrs. White was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
  - Mr. Green was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.
  - Miss Scarlet was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.
  - Monsieur Brunette was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.
  - None of these suspects, including Mr. Green, were in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
  - **Outcome**: LOGICAL ERROR DETECTED: BRANCH IS CONTRADICTORY.

- **Contradiction with previous clues:**
  - Colonel Mustard and Sgt. Gray were in the Lounge together at least once.
  - Mr. Green moved from the Trophy Room to the Hall.
  - No contradiction found.
  - **Outcome**: No contradiction.

#### 4. Manhattan Falsification

- **Manhattan Distances:**
  - Mr. Green moved from the Trophy Room (0, 2) to the Hall (3, 0) at 12:00 AM.
  - Manhattan Distance: |3 - 0| + |0 - 2| = 3 + 2 = 5.
  - **Outcome**: PHYSICAL VIOLATION DETECTED.

   - Mr. Green: Power
   - Sgt. Gray: Betrayal
   - Mrs. White: Pride

- Violates the clue where Mr. Green was in the Courtyard at 12:15 AM

### Step-by-Step Deduction

1. **Motive Clues:**
   - Mr. Green: Power (Motive)
   - Mrs. White: Pride (Motive)
   - Mrs. Peacock: Not specified, so we need more clues to confirm.
   - Monsieur Brunette: Not specified, so we need more clues to confirm.
   - Sgt. Gray: Betrayal (Motive)

2. **Weapon Location at 12:00 AM:**
   - **The Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM if and only if the Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.**
   - **The Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM if and only if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.**
   - Thus, the Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.

3. **Suspect Locations and Movement:**
   - **Mr. Green:**
     - 11:45 PM: Carriage House or Ballroom.
     - 12:00 AM: Courtyard.
     - 12:15 AM: Not specified.
   - **Mrs. White:**
     - 11:45 PM: Not specified, Pride (Motive).
     - 12:00 AM: Trophy Room or not in the Trophy Room.
   - **Sgt. Gray:**
     - 11:45 PM: Studio.
     - 12:00 AM: Lounge.
     - 12:15 AM: Not specified.

     - 11:45 PM: Carriage House.
     - 12:15 AM: Carriage House.
   - **Monsieur Brunette:**
     - 11:45 PM: Carriage House or Ballroom.
     - 12:15 AM: Gazebo.

4. **Location of Mr. Boddy:**
   - **The Rope was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.**
   - **The Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM if and only if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.**
   - **The Candlestick was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM if and only if a suspect motivated by Pride was in the Lounge at 11:45 PM.**
   - Thus, if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM, then the Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM, and Mrs. White was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM. 

### Testing Clues

1. **Assume Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM:**
   - **Wrench in Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.**
   - **Poison in Hall at 12:00 AM.**
   - **Mrs. White in Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.**
   - **Candlestick in Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.**
   - **Pride (Motive) applied to Mrs. White.**
   - **Betrayal (Motive) applied to Mrs. Peacock.**
   - **Power (Motive) applied to Mr. Green.**

2. **Assume Mr. Boddy was not in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM:**
   - This contradicts several clues, including the Cord in the Carriage House and the Wrench location. Therefore, this branch fails.

### Step-by-Step Deduction

#### 1. Mr. Boddy was murdered at 12:00 AM.
- Time: 12:00 AM
- Room (suspect and Mr. Boddy): All suspects and Mr. Boddy must be in the same room with at least one weapon.
- This eliminates **E**, **F**, and **G** until verified.

#### 2. Mr. Green is in the Courtyard at 12:00 AM.
- Mr. Green is not in the same room as Mr. Boddy at 12:00 AM, which means the murder did not occur at these times.
- Time: 12:00 AM
- Room: Not determined yet, but Mr. Green is not involved.

#### 3. The Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM if and only if the Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.
- If the Poison was in the Hall at 12:00 AM, the Wrench must be in the Cloak Room.
- If the Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM, the Poison must be in the Hall at 12:00 AM.

#### 4. Mr. Green was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.
- This means the Lead Pipe, if in the Ballroom at 11:45 PM, must be considered.

#### 5. Colonel Mustard and Sgt. Gray were in the Lounge together at least once.
- Both suspects must be in the Lounge at some point.

#### 6. The Candlestick was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM if and only if a suspect motivated by Pride was in the Lounge at 11:45 PM.
- If a suspect motivated by Pride was in the Lounge at 11:45 PM, the Candlestick must be in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.

#### 7. Mrs. White is motivated by Pride.

#### 8. The Wrench was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM if and only if Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
- If Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM, the Wrench must be in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.

#### 9. The Rope was in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM.
- Monsieur Brunette was in the Carriage House, and he moved the Rope to the Gazebo at 12:15 AM.
- The Rope is in the Gazebo at 12:15 AM.

#### 10. The Knife was in the room just west of the Horseshoe at 11:45 PM.
- This places two suspects who are west of the Horseshoe.

#### 11. The suspect motivated by Power moved from the Fountain to the Carriage House at 12:00 AM.
- Mr. Green was in the Courtyard at 12:00 AM, so the Power suspect moved from the Fountain to the Carriage House.

### Hypothesis Testing

#### Branch 1: Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
- C1 at 11:45 PM: Trophy Room
- Mrs. White is in the Lounge at 11:45 PM: Candlestick in Trophy Room at 12:00 AM
- Wrench in Cloak Room at 12:00 AM (no contradiction)
- Mr. Green in the Carriage House at 11:45 PM: Lead Pipe in Ballroom at 11:45 PM (requires verification)

#### Branch 2: Mr. Boddy was not in the Trophy Room at 11:45 PM.
- No Wrench in Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.
- No Candlestick in Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.
- More dynamic movements to test.

### Suspect Timeline

1. **Sgt. Gray:**

   - Col. Mustard and Sgt. Gray in Lounge at least once.
